HFT visibility is governed by the authoring policy above.","docket_mode":"quarantine_without_hft","docket_ready":true}},"selected_context_units":[],"support_registry":[{"branch_refs":["root_000047/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_1d2d4a4b2397e68ff566","text":"1. **Devotional orientation completes the protective sequence.** ء ل ه `B001` gives `إِلَٰهِ` worship, devotion, and the one treated as the object of worship. Structurally, it completes the progression `رَبِّ` (care), `مَلِكِ` (rule), `إِلَٰهِ` (devotion) immediately before the evil is specified. The packet supplies that order; I infer nurture and governance -> a settled orientation of allegiance that covert whispering seeks to disturb. The phrase therefore reads as the culminating protective relation, not merely a third synonymous title.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_49cdca5edfe5b2c76eea","text":"2. **The fixed divine name becomes a human relation.** The same ء ل ه root appeared in 114:0 as `ٱللَّهِ`, activating `B002` (fixed name in invocation formulas), and now appears as `إِلَٰهِ`, directly activating `B001` (the worshipped one). Between them stands `قُلْ أَعُوذُ`, so the root recurrence links named invocation to relational devotion. The packet supplies name, appeal, and title; I infer the recurrence -> identification of the one invoked by name with the one to whom the wavering `ٱلنَّاسِ` orient themselves. `إِلَٰهِ ٱلنَّاسِ` thus closes an invocation arc opened by the basmalah.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000047/B001","root_000047/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_65defbaca8cd15b5a162","text":"2. **Invocation-name completed.** Evidence: ء ل ه B002 in 114:0, Allah as fixed name in invocation formulas; ء ل ه B001 here, worship and the worshipped one; ق و ل B001 from 114:1, uttered speech. Structural cue: the basmalah names Allah before “قل أعوذ,” and 114:3 rearticulates the divine relation as إله الناس. Assigned role: the ayah converts the proper-name invocation into a relational claim over people. Abductive move: the opening formula and the refuge formula are one speech-chain; the named one in the basmalah is the one invoked as worshipped protector here. Reading change: 114:3 reads as the completion of the basmalah’s invocation logic, not only the third item in a list.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000047/B001","root_000047/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_6f331aff76d5c1fec97b","text":"114:5 retrospectively made “إله الناس” the deepest layer of the refuge sequence. Since the whisper works inside the chest, ء ل ه B001 is not simply a title of worship but the contested center of inward allegiance. 114:0 also returned here: ء ل ه B002 in “ٱللَّه” made this ayah feel like the expansion of the opening invocation, while 114:1’s “قل” supplied the uttered form of that invocation. The surprise is that the three titles do not merely accumulate grandeur; they move inward from care and rule to devotion, exactly where the whisper will operate.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_9396466c2b4ba502bde1","text":"114:4-5 made the devotional title intervene earlier than overt worship: the threat begins as hidden speech before it can settle into commitment. 114:6 added a social correction, since human sources can mediate that pressure; rival orientation need not arrive only from a concealed nonhuman agent.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000047/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_9486a79997bf3f0b48a9","text":"3. **The title governs the conversion of whispers into commitments.** ء ل ه `B001` supplies devotion, while contextual ق و ل `B012/B013` distinguishes an unspoken inner saying from a belief or held doctrine, and و س و س `B001` supplies the passing inner whisper. The packet does not state that every whisper becomes belief; I infer repeated hidden saying -> possible settled commitment, with `إِلَٰهِ` serving as the criterion that blocks that conversion into rival allegiance. The fixed ayah consequently reads as protection of worship and conviction at their formative stage, not only as a statement about divine status.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000047/B001"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-1","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_951815f986b5df7a5918","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"114:3:1\",\"root_000047\",[\"B001\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Rablikten ve hükümdarlıktan sonra gelen ilahlık, korunmayı insanın en içteki kulluk ve bağlılık yönelimine kadar taşır; fısıltı tam da bu yönü saptırabilir.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000047/B001","root_000047/B002"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-2","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_a36df8d3ca75315c71b3","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"114:3:1\",\"root_000047\",[\"B001\",\"B002\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Açılışta özel adıyla çağrılan Allah, burada insanların kulluk ettiği ilah olarak ilişki içinde tanımlanır ve başlangıçtaki yakarış tamamlanır.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000047/B001"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-3","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_b4c597d92d7a4f199a40","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"114:3:1\",\"root_000047\",[\"B001\"]]],\"grade\":\"weak\",\"text\":\"İlah unvanı, geçici bir iç fısıltının yerleşik inanç veya rakip bağlılığa dönüşmesini daha başlangıç aşamasında engelleyen ölçüyü de gösterir.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_004965/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_d7969e074fde0fd10c44","text":"3. **People as worshippers under oscillation.** Evidence: ن و س B001, swaying or dangling oscillation; ق و ل B013 from 114:1, belief or held doctrine; و س و س B001, inward whisper. Structural cue: the third repeated الناس is followed by the disclosure of a whisperer that works in the same الناس. Assigned role: الناس are not just subjects but inwardly persuadable worshippers. Abductive move: oscillation in the human field includes doctrinal or devotional wavering because the threat is inner saying.
